- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 1000 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 393,607 input · 42,572 output
